[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

... (showing lines 1-118 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

[Step 3/60]
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];
         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passes at cost 4,624,409 (area 4604, delay 1004ps). Now let me analyze the design:

**Key observations:**
1. Critical path: weight reg → 8×8 multiplier → 32-bit adder → psum reg.
2. The 32-bit accumulator is over-provisioned: max |sum| = 8×(−128×−128) = 131072 = 2^17, so a **19-bit signed accumulator is exact** (never overflows). Sign-extend to 32 at the output — free wiring.
3. Later: carry-save accumulation (CSA is 1 FA deep vs full CPA per PE) could slash delay.

Let me first make a 19-bit-narrowed version (v1):


  -> create_file(filename='tpu_v1_narrow.v', content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, optimized variant v1.\n//\n// Same microarchitecture as the reference logikbench tpu (8x8 PE mesh,\n// per-row input skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline), but the\n// internal partial-sum datapath is narrowed from 32 to IW=19 bits:\n//\n//   Each output is a sum of 8 signed 8x8 products. The extreme case is\n//   8 * (-128 * -128) = +131072 = 2^17 (min is -130048), so a 19-bit signed\n//   accumulator can hold every intermediate AND final value exactly. The\n//   32-bit sign-extended result is formed only at the output ports (pure\n//   wiring), so behavior is bit-exact versus the 32-bit reference.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)  // internal accumulator width (signed, exact)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,        // weight-load shift enable\n    input                 en,        // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        psum_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   psum_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // signed 8x8 product, sign-extended to the accumulator width\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [IW-1:0]   pext = {{(IW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north + pext;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]      w_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]      a_left,\n    output [N*IW-1:0]     c_bot\n   );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*IW +: IW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north(psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south(psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,      // exact internal accumulator width\n    parameter ACCW = 32)    // output width\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                  a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                 c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]    c_data\n   );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //#######################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zero when a_valid low.\n   //#######################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //#######################################################################\n   // Systolic array (narrow IW-bit partial sums).\n   //#######################################################################\n   wire [N*IW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   //#######################################################################\n   // Output de-skew in the narrow domain, then sign-extend to 32 bits.\n   //#######################################################################\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*IW +: IW];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= {IW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                {{(ACCW-IW){cout[IW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //#######################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //#######################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

  -> create_file(filename='tpu_v2_csa.v', content='//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, CSA (carry-save) variant v2.\n//\n// Microarchitecture at the tile boundary is identical to the reference\n// logikbench tpu (8x8 mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output de-skew,\n// 15-cycle valid pipeline, w_valid stall semantics). Internally each column\n// accumulates the 8 partial products in redundant carry-save form:\n//\n//   PE update (one 19-bit full adder, no carry chain):\n//     w[0] <= w_north                        (weight load)\n//     {c,s} <= s + c + sext(a_west * w)      (compute)\n//   Per column a 3-bit sign flip counter absorbs the products\' sign bits\n//   (sum of 8 signed values is exactly representable for any sign pattern),\n//   so the true column sum is always s + 2c, bit-exact versus a 19-bit\n//   binary accumulator (19 signed bits hold every intermediate/final value:\n//   max magnitude 8*128*128 = 2^17).\n//\n// The expensive 19-bit carry-propagate additions happen only at the 8 output\n// columns during the final deskew merge (a few cycles per result row), not in\n// all 64 PEs every cycle. PE critical path = 8x8 multiplier + one FA level.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_csa\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter IW = 19)  // internal accumulator word width\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,        // weight-load shift enable\n    input                 en,        // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input                 c_north,   // CSA carry word in (shifts through on en)\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,   // CSA sum word in\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg            c_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] a = a_east;  // activation aligned with psum (1-cycle reg)\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a) * $signed(w);\n   wire [IW-1:0]   pext = {{(IW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w       <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n         a_east  <= {DW{1\'b0}};\n         c_south <= 1\'b0;\n         s_south <= {IW{1\'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east  <= a_west;\n            c_south <= c_north;\n            s_south <= s_north + cext(pext, c_north);\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\n   function [IW-1:0] cext(input [IW-1:0] p, input c);\n      cext = {p[IW-2:0], c};\n   endfunction\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_csa_col\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 en,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_in,      // CSA sum word from array bottom\n    output [IW-1:0]       s_out,     // sum word into next column\'s merge pipe\n    output [ACCW-1:0]     c32        // finalized 32-bit result word\n   );\n\n   localparam AW = IW + 1;  // signed add width: 20 bits\n\n   // ---- sign flip pipeline (shifts during en only) -------------------------\n   reg [2:0] f [0:1];\n   integer k;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         f[0] <= 3\'d0;\n         f[1] <= 3\'d0;\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         f[0] <= f[0] + {2\'d0, s_in[IW-1]};\n         f[1] <= f[0];\n      end\n   end\n\n   // ---- final 32-bit merge word -------------------------------------------\n   // acc = s + 2c + (f ? -2^IW : 0) accumulated across the column depth.\n   // (f counts sign bits of products already folded into s; sign-bit weight\n   //  2^(IW-1), 8 products -> flip correction f*2^(IW-1) is captured by\n   //  folding at bit IW.)\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_sh = s_in;          // sum word as-is\n   wire [IW:0]   c_sh = {s_in[IW-2:0], s_in[IW-1], 1\'b0}; // 2c at +1 weight...\n\n   // NOTE: words arrive LSB-first: s_in = {unused_sign.. , s[18:0]} where the\n   // actual word alignment is kept by the shift pipes below.\n   // To keep indexing simple we treat s_in[18:0] as the sum word and\n   // c_in (= s_in[18:0] << 1 | cbit) as its carry companion; the companion\n   // travels as a separate shift pipe \'cpipe\'.\n\n   // Sum word pipeline\n   reg [IW-1:0] sp [0:1];\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         sp[0] <= {IW{1\'b0}};\n         sp[1] <= {IW{1\'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         sp[0] <= s_in;\n         sp[1] <= sp[0];\n      end\n   end\n   assign s_out = sp[1];\n\n   // Carry companion pipeline (IW bits: c[18:0], value 2*c aligned)\n   reg [IW-1:0] cp [0:1];\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         cp[0] <= {IW{1\'b0}};\n sext(cp[1]) - f[1]*2^IW\n   wire [AW-1:0] acc_in;\n   reg  [AW-1:0] acc_r;\n   wire [2:0] f_top = f[1];\n   wire [AW-1:0] corr = f_top[2] ? {{(AW-IW-1){1\'b1}}, {(IW){1\'b0}}}\n                    : {{(AW-IW){sp[1][IW-1]}}, sp[1]}\n                         + {{(AW-IW){cp[1][IW-1]}}, cp[1]}\n                         + corr;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        acc_r <= {AW{1\'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        acc_r <= acc_in;\n   end\n\n   // ---- output register -----------------------------------------------------\n   reg [ACCW-1:0] out_r;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        out_r <= {ACCW{1\'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        out_r <= {{(ACCW-AW){acc_in[AW-1]}}, acc_in};\n   end\n   assign c32 = out_r;\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles, zeros when a_valid low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1\'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n //##########################################################################\n   // 8x8 CSA systolic mesh. c_v[i][j] carries the CSA "carry-in bit" word\n   // (one bit per column element, shifts .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .c_north(c_v[i][j]),\n               .s_north(s_v[i][j]),\n //##########################################################################\n   // Per-column CSA->binary merge + output de-skew.\n   // The merge accumulator streams the 8 words of a column result; a column\n   // becomes ready 1 cycle after its last word leaves the array. Columns are\n   // de-skewed by j (column j needs N-1-j extra delay) before the deskew\n   // chain so all 8 columns of a result row emerge together, matching the\n   // reference latency.\n   //##########################################################################\n\n   // Merge column results first (in the CSA domain words are already aligned\n   // per-row: word m of row m arrives at column j exactly when its partial\n   // chain completes, same time-alignment as the binary c_bot of the\n   // reference design).   -> replace_file(filename='tpu_v2_csa.v', content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, carry-save (CSA) variant.\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior is identical to the reference logikbench tpu:\n//  - 8x8 PE mesh, weights shift in from the top while w_valid (stall compute),\n//  - activations skewed per row (row i delayed i cycles, zeros when !a_valid),\n//  - 15-cycle valid pipeline, output deskew (column j delayed 7-j cycles),\n//  - all state frozen while w_valid is high (except the weight shift).\n//\n// Internal optimization: the per-column south-flowing accumulator is kept in\n// redundant carry-save form {s, c}, value = s + c (mod 2^19).   -> create_file(filename='tpu_v3_mac.v', content='//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, fused redundant MAC variant.\n//\n// Same tile-boundary behavior as the reference logikbench tpu (8x8 mesh,\n// row skew, deskew, 15-cycle latency, w_valid stall). Internal datapath:\n//\n// Each PE updates its column\'s carry-save accumulator {s,c} (value s+c,\n// mod 2^19) by FUSING the signed 8x8 multiplication into the CSA tree:\n// the 8 partial-product rows plus s and c (plus one sign-fix bit) are\n// reduced by a balanced tree of 3:2 compressors straight to the next\n// {s,c} pair. There is no intermediate full-precision product add, so the\n// PE critical path is roughly the depth of a multiplier\'s compression tree\n// alone (no final carry-propagate adder anywhere in the PE).\n//\n// Exactness: |sum of 8 signed byte products| <= 8*2^14 = 2^17, so 19-bit\n// signed (mod 2^19) arithmetic never overflows; s+c is resolved once at\n// the array bottom and sign-extended to 32 bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- operand a, sign-extended to IW (used by all product rows) ---------\n   wire [IW-1:0] A  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] nA = ~A;\n\n   // ---- partial-product rows + accumulator rows ---------------------------\n   // a*w = sum_{k=0..6} w[k]*A*2^k + w[7]*(~A+1)*2^7   (mod 2^IW)\n   // The "+1" of the last term is the single-bit row r10.\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0  = {IW{w[0]}} & A;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1  = ({IW{w[1]}} & A) << 1;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r2  = ({IW{w[2]}} & A) << 2;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3  = ({IW{w[3]}} & A) << 3;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r4  = ({IW{w[4]}} & A) << 4;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r5  = ({IW{w[5]}} & A) << 5;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r6  = ({IW{w[6]}} & A) << 6;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r7  = ({IW{w[7]}} & nA) << 7;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r10 = w[7] ? ({{(IW-1){1\'b0}}, 1\'b1}) << 7 : {IW{1\'b0}};\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressor (carry-save adder) ---------------------------------\n   // csa(x,y,z): s = x^y^z ; c = maj(x,y,z) << 1\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_s(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   endfunction\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   // ---- level 1: 11 rows -> 8 ----------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0 = csa_s(r0, r1, r2),        v0 = csa_c(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u1 = csa_s(r3, r4, r5),        v1 = csa_c(r3, r4, r5);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u2 = csa_s(r6, r7, s_north),   v2 = csa_c(r6, r7, s_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u3 = csa_s(c_north, r10, {IW{1\'b0}}),\n                 v3 = csa_c(c_north, r10, {IW{1\'b0}});\n\n   // ---- level 2: 8 rows -> 6 -----------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] p0 = csa_s(u0, v0, u1),        q0 = csa_c(u0, v0, u1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] p1 = csa_s(v1, u2, v2),        q1 = csa_c(v1, u2, v2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] p2 = csa_s(u3, v3, {IW{1\'b0}}),q2 = csa_c(u3, v3, Now a bold optimization: reduce the CSA tree depth by using radix-4 Booth encoding of the stationary weight (rows drop from 11 → 7, tree levels 5 → 4). Let me implement v4:


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, radix-4 Booth MAC variant.\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, row skew, column deskew, 15-cycle latency, w_valid stall).\n//\n// PE datapath: the column accumulator flows south in carry-save form {s,c}\n// (value s+c, mod 2^19). Each cycle the PE adds a*w to it via a fused\n// compressor tree. The signed 8x8 product is generated with radix-4 Booth\n// recoding of the (stationary) weight, producing only 4 signed-digit\n// product rows {0,+/-a,+/-2a}*4^k plus one small correction row, instead\n// of 8 AND rows + sign row. With s and c that is 7 rows, reduced to 2 by\n// a 4-level 3:2 compressor tree (vs 5 levels for 11 rows).\n//\n// Exactness: |sum of 8 products| <= 8*2^14 = 2^17 < 2^18, so mod-2^19\n// arithmetic never wraps; s+c is resolved once at the array bottom and\n// sign-extended to 32 bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0] reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- Booth recoding of w (signed, bits: w[-1]=0, w[8]=w[7]) ------------\n   // digit k: d_k = w[2k] + w[2k-1] - 2*w[2k+1]  (in {-2,-1,0,1,2})\n   wire [1:0] wx = {w[7], w};              // w[8:0] sign-extended index space\n   wire e0n = w[1],             e0s = w[0];                    // k=0: y1, (y0 + y-1=0)\n   wire e1n = w[3], e1a = w[2], e1b = w[1];                    // k=1\n   wire e2n = w[5], e2a = w[4], e2b = w[3];                    // k=2\n   wire e3n = wx[8], e3a = w[6], e3b = w[5];                   // k=3\n\n   wire m1_0 = e0s;                     // mag1 k0: w0 ^ 0\n   wire m2_0 = ~e0s;                    // mag2 k0: w0 xnor 0\n   wire m1_1 = e1a ^ e1b, m2_1 = ~(e1a ^ e1b);\n   wire m1_2 = e2a ^ e2b, m2_2 = ~(e2a ^ e2b);\n   wire m1_3 = e3a ^ e3b, m2_3 = ~(e3a ^ e3b);\n\n   wire nz_0 = m1_0 | (m2_0 & (e0n ^ e0s));\n   wire nz_1 = m1_1 | (m2_1 & (e1n ^ e1a));\n   wire nz_2 = m1_2 | (m2_2 & (e2n ^ e2a));\n   wire nz_3 = m1_3 | (m2_3 & (e3n ^ e3a));\n\n   // ---- activation rows -----------------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};  // sign-extended a\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa2 = {pa[IW-2:0], 1'b0};                 // 2a (mod 2^IW)\n\n   // row_k = ((inv ? ~(base<<2k) : (base<<2k)) with bits <2k cleared) if nz\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = m1_0 ? pa : pa2;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = m1_1 ? pa : pa2;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2 = m1_2 ? pa : pa2;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3 = m1_3 ? pa : pa2;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] wire [IW-1:0] rC = {{(IW-8){1'b0}},\n                       nz_3 & e3n, 1'b0, nz_2 & e2n, 1'b0,\n                       nz_1 & e1n, 1'b0, nz_0 & e0n, 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressor functions csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   // ---- 7 rows -> 2 rows in 4 compressor levels The Booth recoding was wrong. Let me rewrite the PE with correct radix-4 Booth math (verified: digit decode, `-(a<<s) = (~a<<s) + (1<<s)` so corrections land at distinct bit positions).


  -> replace_file(filename='tpu_v4_booth.v', content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, radix-4 Booth MAC variant.\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output deskew, 15-cycle latency,\n// w_valid stall semantics).\n//\n// PE datapath: the per-column accumulator flows south in carry-save form\n// {s,c} (value s+c mod 2^IW). Each compute cycle the PE adds a*w to it by\n// generating the signed 8x8 product with radix-4 Booth recoding of the\n// stationary weight (4 signed-digit rows + one correction row), then\n// reducing {r0,r1,r2,r3,corr,s,c} (7 rows) to a new {s,c} with a 4-level\n// 3:2 compressor tree. There is no final carry-propagate add in the PE.\n//\n// Booth identities used (mod 2^IW):\n//   w = sum_k d_k 4^k,  d_k in {-2..+2}\n//   digit k from triplet (y_{2k+1}, y_{2k}, y_{2k-1}), y_-1=0, y_8=y_7=w[7]\n//   +d: row = a<<s                  ; s = 2k + (|d|==2)\n//   -d: row = ~a<<s, plus +1 at bit s  (since -(a<<s) = (~a<<s) + (1<<s))\n//   Corrections of all negative digits sit at distinct bit positions, so\n//   they form a single sparse row.\n//\n// Exactness: |sum of 8 signed byte products| <= 8*128*128 = 2^17 < 2^18,\n// so mod-2^IW (IW=19) arithmetic never wraps; s+c is resolved once at the\n// array bottom and sign-extended to 32 bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- activation rows -----------------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] npa = ~pa;\n\n   // ---- radix-4 Booth decode of stationary weight w ------------------------\n   // digit k uses (y_{2k+1}, y_{2k}, y_{2k-1})\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   // digit 0: (w1, w0, 0)\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & (w[1] ^ w[0]);\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | dm2[0];\n   // digit 1: (w3, w2, w1)\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | dm2[1];\n   // digit 2: (w5, w4, w3)\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | dm2[2];\n   // digit 3: (w7, w6, w5)\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | dm2[3];\n\n   // ---- Booth partial-product rows ------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = dneg[0] ? npa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = dneg[1] ? npa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2 = dneg[2] ? npa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3 = dneg[3] ? npa : pa;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = (dm2[0] ? (b2 << 5) : (b2 << 4)) & {IW{dnz[2]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3 = (dm2[3] ? //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; inject zero when !a_valid.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n       end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // 8x8 Booth-MAC CSA systolic mesh.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   c_v [ASSISTANT]
Now I have a working Booth-MAC design (cost 2,612,116). Let me push further with two optimizations:

1. **v5**: Ones'-complement trick — use unmasked `~(a<<s)` rows for negative Booth digits (each introduces exactly −1 error mod 2^19), track the count of negative digits in a small 6-bit south-flowing counter, correct once at the bottom. This removes the correction row → 6 rows → **3-level** CSA tree instead of 4.


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, radix-4 Booth MAC, 6-row\n// 3-level compressor tree variant (v5).\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output deskew, 15-cycle latency,\n// w_valid stall semantics).\n//\n// PE datapath: per-column accumulator flows south in carry-save form {s,c}\n// (value s+c mod 2^IW). The signed 8x8 product a*w is Booth-recoded on the\n// stationary weight (4 signed digits). Negative digits use the row\n// ~(a<<s) WITHOUT the +1 two's-complement correction: in mod-2^IW\n// arithmetic ~(a<<s) = -(a<<s) - 1, i.e. each negative digit row carries\n// an error of exactly -1. A tiny 6-bit counter flows south alongside the\n// accumulator counting negative digits (max 4/product * 8 products = 32),\n// and the single +n correction is applied once at the array bottom:\n//     result = s + c + n_neg   (mod 2^IW, exact since |sum| <= 2^17)\n//\n// This removes the correction row, leaving only 6 rows (4 Booth rows + s + c)\n// reduced to a new {s,c} by a 3-level 3:2 compressor tree (vs 4 levels).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    input [5:0]           n_north,      // negative-digit counter from above\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south,\n    output reg [5:0]      n_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- activation rows -----------------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] npa = ~pa;\n\n   // ---- radix-4 Booth decode of stationary weight w ------------------------\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & (w[1] ^ w[0]);\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | dm2[0];\n\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | dm2[1];\n\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | dm2[2];\n\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | dm2[3];\n\n   // ---- Booth rows (negative rows unmasked: error -1 each) ------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = dneg[0] ? npa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = dneg[1] ? npa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2 = dneg[2] ? npa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3 = dneg[3] ? npa : pa;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = (dm2[0] ? (b0 << 1) :  b0      ) & {IW{dnz[0]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1 = (dm2[1] ? (b1 << 3) : (b1 << 2)) & {IW{dnz[1]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r2 = (dm2[2] ? (b2 << 5) : (b2 << 4)) & {IW{dnz[2]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3 = (dm2[3] ? (b3 << 7) : (b3 << 6)) & {IW{dnz[3]}};\n\n   // ---- negative digit count for this product --------------------------------\n   wire [2:0] np = (dneg[0] & dnz[0]) + (dneg[1] & dnz[1])\n v5 (2.72M) is slightly worse than v4 (2.61M) — the counter FFs cost more than the saved tree level. Reverting to v4 as the base. Now let me try **v6: store Booth-decoded control bits in the PE weight register** (12 FFs instead of 8 raw-byte FFs), removing the decode from the compute critical path; raw byte reconstructed only during weight-load shifting:


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, v6:\n// stored radix-4 Booth decode + 7-row 4-level CSA fused MAC.\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output deskew, 15-cycle latency,\n// w_valid stall semantics).\n//\n// Key micro-optimizations:\n//  * The stationary weight is stored in Booth-DECODED form: per PE, four\n//    digits {dneg, dm2, dnz} (12 bits) instead of the raw byte. During\n//    weight load the south-going raw shift chain is rebuilt combinationally\n//    from the stored digits (w = sum d_k 4^k).\n//  * Each compute cycle the PE adds a*w to the south-flowing carry-save\n//    accumulator {s,c} (value s+c mod 2^19) by forming 4 Booth product\n//    rows + 1 sparse correction row from the stored decode and the incoming\n//    activation, then reducing {r0,r1,r2,r3,corr,s,c} to a new {s,c} with\n//    a 4-level 3:2 compressor tree. No carry-propagate add inside the PE.\n//  * Exactness: |sum of 8 signed byte products| <= 2^17 < 2^18, so mod-2^19\n//    arithmetic never wraps; s+c is resolved once per column at the array\n//    bottom and sign-extended to 32 bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,    // raw weight byte in (load chain)\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,    // raw weight byte out (load chain)\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   // ---- stored Booth decode of the stationary weight -----------------------\n   // digit k (k=0..3): value d_k = y_{2k} + y_{2k-1} - 2*y_{2k+1}, w-bits\n   // with y_-1 = 0 and y_8 = w[7].  Stored controls:\n   //   dneg[k]: digit is negative and nonzero (row = ~(pa<<s), +corr bit)\n   //   dm2[k] : |digit| == 2 (row shifted one extra)\n   //   dnz[k] : digit != 0   (row enabled)\n   reg [3:0] dneg_r, dm2_r, dnz_r;\n\n   // decode of a raw byte (used at load time)\n   wire [3:0] dneg_c, dm2_c, dnz_c;\n   assign dneg_c[0] = w_north[1];\n   assign dm2_c[0]  = ~w_north[0] & (w_north[1] ^ w_north[0]);\n   assign dnz_c[0]  = w_north[0] | dm2_c[0];\n\n   assign dneg_c[1] = w_north[3] & ~(w_north[2] & w_north[1]);\n   assign dm2_c[1]  = (w_north[2] ~^ w_north[1]) & (w_north[3] ^ w_north[2]);\n   assign dnz_c[1]  = (w_north[2] ^ w_north[1]) | dm2_c[1];\n\n   assign dneg_c[2] = w_north[5] & ~(w_north[4] & w_north[3]);\n   assign dm2_c[2]  = (w_north[4] ~^ w_north[3]) & (w_north[5] ^ w_north[4]);\n   assign dnz_c[2]  = (w_north[4] ^ w_north[3]) | dm2_c[2];\n\n   assign dneg_c[3] = w_north[7] & ~(w_north[6] & w_north[5]);\n   assign dm2_c[3]  = (w_north[6] ~^ w_north[5]) & (w_north[7] ^ w_north[6]);\n   assign dnz_c[3]  = (w_north[6] ^ w_north[5]) | dm2_c[3];\n\n   // reconstruct raw byte from stored digits for the load shift chain:\n   // w = d0 + 4*d1 + 16*d2 + 64*d3  (signed digits in {-2..+2})\n   wire signed [3:0] d0 = dneg_r[0] ? (dm2_r[0] ? -4'sd2 : -4'sd1)\n                                    : (dnz_r[0] ? (dm2_r[0] ? 4'sd2 : 4'sd1) : 4'sd0);\n   wire signed [3:0] d1 = dneg_r[1] ? (dm2_r[1] ? -4'sd2 : -4'sd1)\n                                    : (dnz_r[1] ? (dm2_r[1] ? 4'sd2 : 4'sd1) : 4'sd0);\n   wire signed [3:0] d2 = dneg_r[2] ? (dm2_r[2] ? -4'sd2 : -4'sd1)\n                                    : (dnz_r[2] ? (dm2_r[2] ? 4'sd2 : 4'sd1) : 4'sd0);\n   wire signed [3:0] d3 = dneg_r[3] ? (dm2_r[3] ? -4'sd2 : -4'sd1)\n                                    : (dnz_r[3] ? (dm2_r[3] ? 4'sd2 : 4'sd1) : 4'sd0);\n   wire signed [9:0] wrec = (d3 <<< 6) + (d2 <<< 4) + (d1 <<< 2) + d0;\n   assign w_south = wrec[7:0];\n\n   // ---- activation ------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n\n   // ---- Booth rows from stored decode -----------------------------------------\n   // row_k = dnz ? (dneg ? ~(pa << (2k+dm2)) : (pa << (2k+dm2))) : 0\n   wire [IW-1:0] p0 = dm2_r[0] ?    endfunction
   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);

  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, v7:\n// radix-4 Booth MAC with ones'-complement negative rows, 6 rows -> 2 in a\n// 3-level 3:2 compressor tree (no correction row inside the PE).\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output deskew, 15-cycle latency,\n// w_valid stall semantics).\n//\n// PE datapath: south-flowing carry-save accumulator {s,c} (value s+c,\n// mod 2^IW). Product a*w is Booth-recoded on w. Row generation:\n//   row_k = (dnz ? (p_k ^ {IW{dneg}}) : 0),  p_k = pa << (2k + dm2)\n// For negative digits this yields ~(pa<<s) = -(pa<<s) - 1 (mod 2^IW):\n// each negative row carries an error of exactly -1. A 6-bit counter chain\n// (n, max 32) flows south counting negative digits; the bottom-of-column\n// resolve adds it back: result = s + c + n (exact, |sum| <= 2^17).\n//\n// Rows into the tree: r0,r1,r2,r3,s,c = 6 -> 3-level reduction.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    input [5:0]           n_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south,\n    output reg [5:0]      n_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- activation row ------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n\n   // ---- radix-4 Booth decode of w --------------------------------------------\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & (w[1] ^ w[0]);\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | dm2[0];\n\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | dm2[1];\n\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | dm2[2];\n\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | dm2[3];\n\n   // ---- Booth rows (negation before shift; negative rows unmasked => -1 err) -\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = ((dm2[0] ? (pa << 1) :  pa      ) ^ {IW{dneg[0]}}) & {IW{dnz[0]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1 = ((dm2[1] ? (pa << 3) : (pa << 2)) ^ {IW{dneg[1]}}) & {IW{dnz[1]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r2 = ((dm2[2] ? (pa << 5) : (pa << 4)) ^ {IW{dneg[2]}}) & {IW{dnz[2]}};\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3 = ((dm2[3] ? (pa << 7) : (pa << 6)) ^ {IW{dneg[3]}}) & {IW{dnz[3]}};\n\n   // ---- per-product negative-digit count --------------------------------------\n   wire [2:0] np = {1'b0, dneg[0], 1'b0} + {1'b0, dneg[1], 1'b0}\n                 + {1'b0, dneg[2], 1'b0} + {1'b0, dneg[3], 1'b0};\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressor helpers --------------------------------------------------\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_s(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   endfunction\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   // ---- 6 rows -> 2 rows in 3 compressor levels ---------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0 = csa_s(r0, r1, r2),         v0 = csa_c(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u1 = csa_s(r3, s_north, c_north),\n                 v1 = csa_c(r3, s_north, c_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] x0 = csa_s(u0, v0, u1),         y0 = csa_c(u0, v0, u1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_new = csa_s(x0, y0, v1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c_new = csa_c(x0, y0, v1);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w       <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         s_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n         c_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n         n_south <= 6'd0;\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east  <= a_west;\n            s_south <= s_new;\n            c_south <= c_new;\n            n_south <= n_north + np;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                  a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                 c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]    c_data\n   );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; inject zero when !a_valid.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // 8x8 Booth-MAC CSA systolic mesh with negative-digit counters.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [5:0]      n_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign n_v[0][j] = 6'd0;\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe_mac #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north(s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north(c_v[i][j]),\n               .n_north(n_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south(s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south(c_v[i+1][j]),\n               .n_south(n_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Column resolve (s + c + n) + output deskew + sign extension.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] m19 [0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_resolve\n         assign m19[j] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j] + {{(IW-6){1'b0}}, n_v[N][j]};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = m19[j];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= {IW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-IW){cout[IW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v7.v')
