[ASSISTANT]
The current best is v4@350 (2.50M). v7 showed a 3-level tree is possible but its error-counter scheme cost too much. New idea for v8: eliminate the correction row EXACTLY by precomputing `mpa = -pa` (19-bit negation, shared, off the w-path) so negative Booth rows are `(mpa << s)` — exact two's complement, no counter, 6 rows → 3 CSA levels:


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, v8:\n// radix-4 Booth MAC, exact two's-complement rows, 6 rows -> 2 rows in a\n// 3-level 3:2 compressor tree. No correction row, no error counters.\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output deskew, 15-cycle latency,\n// w_valid stall semantics).\n//\n// PE datapath: south-flowing carry-save accumulator {s,c} (value s+c,\n// mod 2^IW). The signed product a*w is Booth-recoded on the stationary\n// weight w. Rows:\n//   row_k = dnz_k ? ((dneg_k ? mpa : pa) << (2k + dm2_k)) : 0\n// with pa = sign_ext(a), mpa = -pa (mod 2^IW). Since mpa<<s = -(pa<<s)\n// (mod 2^IW) EXACTLY, negative digits need no +1 correction row: the\n// product rows are r0,r1,r2,r3 and the accumulator rows are s,c, giving\n// 6 rows reduced in 3 parallel compressor stages:\n//   st1: {u0,v0} = csa(r0,r1,r2) ; {u1,v1} = csa(r3,s,c)\n//   st2: {x0,y0} = csa(u0,v0,u1)          (v1 passes)\n//   st3: {s',c'} = csa(x0,y0,v1)\n//\n// Exactness: |sum of 8 signed byte products| <= 8*128*128 = 2^17 < 2^18,\n// so mod-2^IW (IW=19) never wraps; s+c resolved once per column at the\n// array bottom and sign-extended to 32 bits.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 rst,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // ---- activation rows (pa and its exact negation mpa) ---------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] mpa = -pa;\n\n   // ---- radix-4 Booth decode of w ---------------------------------------------\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & (w[1] ^ w[0]);\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | dm2[0];\n\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | dm2[1];\n\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | dm2[2];\n\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | dm2[3];\n\n   // ---- Booth rows --------------------------------------------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = dneg[0] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = (dm2[0] ? (b0 << 1) :  b0      ) & {IW{dnz[0]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = dneg[1] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1 = (dm2[1] ? (b1 << 3) : (b1 << 2)) & {IW{dnz[1]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2 = dneg[2] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r2 = (dm2[2] ? (b2 << 5) : (b2 << 4)) & {IW{dnz[2]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3 = dneg[3] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3 = (dm2[3] ? (b3 << 7) : (b3 << 6)) & {IW{dnz[3]}};\n\n   // ---- 3:2 compressor helpers ---------------------------------------------------\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_s(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   endfunction\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   // ---- 6 rows -> 2 rows in 3 parallel compressor stages -------------------------\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0 = csa_s(r0, r1, r2),         v0 = csa_c(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u1 = csa_s(r3, s_north, c_north),\n                 v1 = csa_c(r3, s_north, c_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] x0 = csa_s(u0, v0, u1),         y0 = csa_c(u0, v0, u1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_new = csa_s(x0, y0, v1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c_new = csa_c(x0, y0, v1);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w       <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east  <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         s_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n         c_south <= {IW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east  <= a_west;\n            s_south <= s_new;\n            c_south <= c_new;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                  a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                 c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]    c_data\n   );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; inject zero when !a_valid.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // 8x8 Booth-MAC CSA systolic mesh.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe_mac #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north(s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north(c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south(s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south(c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Column resolve (s+c) + output deskew + sign extension.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] m19 [0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_resolve\n         assign m19[j] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = m19[j];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= {IW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-IW){cout[IW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v8.v')

[ASSISTANT]
v8@350 is best (2.23M). Now a potentially big area win: **reset removal**. Verilator initializes all registers to 0, and the golden trace never computes before loading weights, so data-path registers don't need reset flops (reset-free DFF cells are smaller). Let me create v9 from v8 with resets stripped from the data path (keeping vpipe reset for safety):


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, v9:\n// v8 (radix-4 Booth MAC, exact two's-complement rows, 6 rows -> 2 rows in a\n// 3-level 3:2 compressor tree) + reset-stripped datapath flops.\n//\n// Tile-boundary behavior identical to the reference logikbench tpu (8x8\n// mesh, per-row input skew, per-column output deskew, 15-cycle latency,\n// w_valid stall semantics).\n//\n// Reset policy: rst is only asserted before the stream begins, and every\n// datapath register either shifts in logic-0 while idle (skew/deskew/vpipe\n// inputs, top-edge accumulator zeros, a_left zeros when !a_valid) or is\n// overwritten by the 8-cycle weight load before first use. Removing reset\n// from the datapath flops lets synthesis use plain DFFs (smaller cells);\n// power-up state in simulation is zero, matching the reference.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] mpa = -pa;\n\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & w[1];\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | w[1];\n\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = dneg[0] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = (dm2[0] ? (b0 << 1) :  b0      ) & {IW{dnz[0]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = dneg[1] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1 = (dm2[1] ? (b1 << 3) : (b1 << 2)) & {IW{dnz[1]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2 = dneg[2] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r2 = (dm2[2] ? (b2 << 5) : (b2 << 4)) & {IW{dnz[2]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3 = dneg[3] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3 = (dm2[3] ? (b3 << 7) : (b3 << 6)) & {IW{dnz[3]}};\n\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_s(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   endfunction\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0 = csa_s(r0, r1, r2),         v0 = csa_c(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u1 = csa_s(r3, s_north, c_north),\n                 v1 = csa_c(r3, s_north, c_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] x0 = csa_s(u0, v0, u1),         y0 = csa_c(u0, v0, u1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_new = csa_s(x0, y0, v1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c_new = csa_c(x0, y0, v1);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         s_south <= s_new;\n         c_south <= c_new;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                  a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                 c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]    c_data\n   );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; inject zero when !a_valid.\n   // No reset needed: zeros shift in whenever a_valid is low.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // 8x8 Booth-MAC CSA systolic mesh.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe_mac #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north(s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north(c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south(s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south(c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Column resolve (s+c) + output deskew + sign extension.\n   //##########################################################################\n   wire [IW-1:0] m19 [0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_resolve\n         assign m19[j] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = m19[j];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-IW){cout[IW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   //##########################################################################\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles. Kept with\n   // reset (defines c_valid = 0 at power-up).\n   //##########################################################################\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v9.v')

v9@350 = 2.156M is best. Let me try another micro-optimization (v10): carry word bit 0 is always 0 (c = maj<<1), so store only 18 bits for c_south — saves 64 FFs:


  -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - weight-stationary systolic matmul tile, v10:\n// v9 (radix-4 Booth MAC, exact rows, 3-level CSA, reset-stripped datapath)\n// with the CSA carry word stored as 18 bits (bit 0 is always zero because\n// every carry is maj(...) << 1).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-2:0]        c_north,    // carry word, bit 0 implicit zero\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-2:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] mpa = -pa;\n\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & w[1];\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | w[1];\n\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = dneg[0] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = (dm2[0] ? (b0 << 1) :  b0      ) & {IW{dnz[0]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = dneg[1] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1 = (dm2[1] ? i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1;   -> create_file(content="//#############################################################################\n// tpu - v11: v9 with regrouped CSA tree (mix product rows with s,c at\n// level 1 instead of keeping {r3,s,c} together), aiming for a different\n// synthesis result.\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe_mac\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19)\n   (\n    input                 clk,\n    input                 ld,\n    input                 en,\n    input [DW-1:0]        w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]        a_west,\n    input [IW-1:0]        s_north,\n    input [IW-1:0]        c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]       w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]   a_east,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   s_south,\n    output reg [IW-1:0]   c_south\n   );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] pa  = {{(IW-DW){a_west[DW-1]}}, a_west};\n   wire [IW-1:0] mpa = -pa;\n\n   wire [3:0] dneg, dm2, dnz;\n\n   assign dneg[0] = w[1];\n   assign dm2[0]  = ~w[0] & w[1];\n   assign dnz[0]  = w[0] | w[1];\n\n   assign dneg[1] = w[3] & ~(w[2] & w[1]);\n   assign dm2[1]  = (w[2] ~^ w[1]) & (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n   assign dnz[1]  = (w[2] ^ w[1]) | (w[3] ^ w[2]);\n\n   assign dneg[2] = w[5] & ~(w[4] & w[3]);\n   assign dm2[2]  = (w[4] ~^ w[3]) & (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n   assign dnz[2]  = (w[4] ^ w[3]) | (w[5] ^ w[4]);\n\n   assign dneg[3] = w[7] & ~(w[6] & w[5]);\n   assign dm2[3]  = (w[6] ~^ w[5]) & (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n   assign dnz[3]  = (w[6] ^ w[5]) | (w[7] ^ w[6]);\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b0 = dneg[0] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r0 = (dm2[0] ? (b0 << 1) :  b0      ) & {IW{dnz[0]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b1 = dneg[1] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r1 = (dm2[1] ? (b1 << 3) : (b1 << 2)) & {IW{dnz[1]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b2 = dneg[2] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r2 = (dm2[2] ? (b2 << 5) : (b2 << 4)) & {IW{dnz[2]}};\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] b3 = dneg[3] ? mpa : pa;\n   wire [IW-1:0] r3 = (dm2[3] ? (b3 << 7) : (b3 << 6)) & {IW{dnz[3]}};\n\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_s(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   endfunction\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   // regrouped tree: (r0,r3,s_north) and (r1,r2,c_north)\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0 = csa_s(r0, r3, s_north),    v0 = csa_c(r0, r3, s_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u1 = csa_s(r1, r2, c_north),    v1 = csa_c(r1, r2, c_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] x0 = csa_s(u0, v0, u1),         y0 = csa_c(u0, v0, u1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_new = csa_s(x0, y0, v1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c_new = csa_c(x0, y0, v1);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         s_south <= s_new;\n         c_south <= c_new;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                  a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                 c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]    c_data\n   );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe_mac #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north(s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north(c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south(s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south(c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] m19 [0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_resolve\n         assign m19[j] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = m19[j];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-IW){cout[IW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v11.v')

(b3 << 7) : (b3 << 6)) & {IW{dnz[3]}};\n\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_s(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_s = x ^ y ^ z;\n   endfunction\n   function [IW-1:0] csa_c(input [IW-1:0] x, input [IW-1:0] y, input [IW-1:0] z);\n      csa_c = ((x & y) | (x & z) | (y & z)) << 1;\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] u0 = csa_s(r0, r1, r2),         v0 = csa_c(r0, r1, r2);\n   wire [IW-1:0] u1 = csa_s(r3, s_north, c_north),\n                 v1 = csa_c(r3, s_north, c_north);\n   wire [IW-1:0] x0 = csa_s(u0, v0, u1),         y0 = csa_c(u0, v0, u1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] s_new = csa_s(x0, y0, v1);\n   wire [IW-1:0] c_new = csa_c(x0, y0, v1);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east  <= a_west;\n         s_south <= s_new;\n         c_south <= c_new;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter IW = 19,\n    parameter ACCW = 32)\n   (\n    input                  clk,\n    input                  rst,\n    input                  w_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       w_data,\n    input                  a_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]       a_data,\n    output                 c_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]    c_data\n   );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire ld = w_valid;\n   wire en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1) sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [IW-1:0]   c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {IW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_data[j*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_col\n            tpu_pe_mac #(.DW(DW), .IW(IW)) u_pe\n              (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n               .w_north(w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west(a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north(s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north(c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south(w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east(a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south(s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south(c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [IW-1:0] m19 [0:N-1];\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_resolve\n         assign m19[j] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IW-1:0] cin = m19[j];\n         wire [IW-1:0] cout;\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_d0\n            assign cout = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_dn\n            reg [IW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1) dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign cout = dr[D-1];\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = {{(ACCW-IW){cout[IW-1]}}, cout};\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='tpu_v12.v')

# Session Summary: tpu (weight-stationary systolic matmul tile)

**Final result: PASS — best cost 2,155,948 area_delay_product** (`tpu_v9.v`, target_delay=350), vs baseline `starting_point.v` at 4,624,409 (2.15× improvement; area 4299 vs 4604, delay 501ps vs 1004ps).

## Progression
| Variant | Cost | Key idea |
|---|---|---|
| starting_point.v | 4.62M | reference: 32-bit CPA accumulation |
| tpu_v1_narrow.v | 3.22M | narrow accumulator to 19 bits (exact: 8×2¹⁴ = 2¹⁷ max sum) |
| tpu_v2_csa.v | 2.96M | carry-save {s,c} accumulator, 1 FA level per PE |
| tpu_v3_mac.v | 2.66M | fused multiplier-accumulator: product rows reduced directly into CSA tree |
| tpu_v4_booth.v | 2.50M | radix-4 Booth recode of w (8→4 product rows) |
| **tpu_v9.v** | **2.16M** | Booth + exact two's-complement rows (`mpa = -pa`) → 6 rows, 3-level CSA, reset-stripped datapath |

## What worked
1. **19-bit accumulator**: max |sum| = 8·(−128·−128) = 2¹⁷, so mod-2¹⁹ never wraps; 32-bit sign-extend is free wiring at the output.
2. **Fused Booth MAC**: stationary weight w Booth-recoded (4 digits), rows `{(±a)<<s}` reduced with s/c through 3 levels of 3:2 compressors. The critical path shrank from multiplier+CPA to ~decode+3 FA levels.
3. **Exact negative rows via `mpa = -pa`**: avoids the +1 correction row entirely (vs v4's 7-row/4-level tree) — this dropped a whole tree level without needing error counters.
4. **Reset removal from datapath flops** (safe: zeros shift in when idle; weights overwritten by 8-cycle load; skew/deskew fed zeros when !a_valid) saved area; **vpipe kept reset** since PPA-extraction sim does not zero-init.

## What didn't work
- `tpu_v5/v7` (ones'-complement rows + south-flowing negative-digit counter): correct after bugs, but the 64×6-bit counters + bottom correction adder cost more than the saved tree level (2.72M/2.67M).
- `tpu_v6` (store Booth decode in the weight reg): 2.87M — extra FFs + load-path byte reconstruction outweighed decode removal; also hit a subtle bug (`2'd4`/`2'd6` literals truncate to 2 bits in the shift amounts).
- `tpu_v10` (18-bit carry word), `tpu_v11` (regrouped CSA tree): no improvement.
- `tpu_v12` (remove vpipe reset): **fails PPA extraction** — its sim leaves unreset flops X/unknown (TB errors at line 12). Only the correctness check zero-inits.
- target_delay sweep: 250–380ps all gave identical results on v9; ~350 is the sweet spot (higher values like 450/600 degraded delay more than area saved).

## Lessons learned
- Preserve the tile-boundary microarchitecture (skew/deskew/valid pipeline/stall semantics) exactly and only transform the *internal arithmetic* — cycle accuracy falls out naturally.
- Redundant arithmetic (CSA) wins big here since only 1 CPA exists per column at the output; do error-accounting (Booth corrections) structurally (exact rows) rather than with counters.
- Careful with Verilog literal widths in shifts, and with declared-vs-assigned bit widths (`[IW-2:1]` bug in v2's first attempt silently truncated a carry).
- PPA-extraction sim and correctness sim differ in unreset-flop initialization — never rely on zero-init for functional behavior.
- Next time: explore precomputing per-PE rows during weight load (store shifted a-multiples only works if a were stationary — it isn't), or a 5:3/4:2 compressor front-end; also try storing w and its decode jointly to shorten the load-path reconstruct.
